<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屡次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>收点</rb><rt>siu-tin</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>文雅</rb><rt>veng-yüô</rt><rb>粗蛮</rb><rt>tsʽu-mæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>债户</rb><rt>tsa-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尽</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心里个</rb><rt>*sing-li-go*</rt><rb>忖头</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>*bih-nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>*bih-nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>*Sing-shü*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>呒周割礼</rb><rt>m-tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>*lih-fah-go*</rt><rb>文字</rb><rt>veng-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>肉身上</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>*tsiu-keh-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>心里个</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>灵气</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>文字</rb><rt>veng-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>称赞个</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. III.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>*ih-cʽü*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>多个</rb><rt>to-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>诏书</rb><rt>tsiao-shü</rt><rb>托付</rb><rt>tʽôh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呒信实个</rb><rt>m-sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒信实</rb><rt>m-sing-jih</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>信实</rb><rt>sing-jih</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>𣍐个</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>说谎个</rb><rt>shih-hwông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚实个</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>动怒</rb><rt>dong-nu</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>𣍐个</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>*ziah-z*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>*tsing-dzih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，）「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公平个</rb><rt>kong-bing-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好如</rb><rt>hao-jü</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>*bih-nying*</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>Ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坐罪了个</rb><rt>zo-ze-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>Tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>岔路</rb><rt>tsʽô-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>变了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>无用个</rb><rt>vu-yüong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>良善</rb><rt>liang-jün</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡咙</rb><rt>wu-long</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>开间个</rb><rt>kʽæ-kæn-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>单</rb><rt>*tæn*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘴唇</rb><rt>cü-jing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>蛇个</rb><rt>dzô-go</rt><rb>毒气</rb><rt>doh-kyʽi</rt><rb>囥的</rb><rt>kʽông-tih</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>满口</rb><rt>mun-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>恶毒</rb><rt>ôh-doh</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>飞跑</rb><rt>fi-pʽao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打出</rb><rt>tang-cʽih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt><rb>患难</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>太平个</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼面前</rb><rt>ngæn-min-zin</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下个</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>塞住</rb><rt>seh-djü</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服罪</rb><rt>voh-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kao-hyüing. 或话，教训。（原注）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>NÆN-KÆN</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>表出来了</rb><rt>piao-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>对证个</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>宽恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>眼开眼闭</rb><rt>ngæn-kʽæ-ngæn-pi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>犯过个</rb><rt>væn-ko-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>除掉了</rb><rt>djü-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>除掉个</rb><rt>*djü-diao-go*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>行为个</rb><rt>ʽang-we-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>*fông-fah*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可见</rb><rt>kʽo-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>*tsing-dzih*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. IV.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>*kwʽô-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？「<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>作工个</rb><rt>tsôh-kong-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>盖住了</rb><rt>kæ-djü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>呒周割礼个</rb><rt>m-tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>才始</rb><rt>*dzæ-s*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>落空了</rb><rt>lôh-kʽong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>变了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>呒用场</rb><rt>m-yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>国份个</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒想望</rb><rt>m-siang-vông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>想望个</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>国份个</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>*Sing-shü*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>*to*</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>靠百岁</rb><rt>kʽao-pah-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>枯燥了</rb><rt>kwʽu-sao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>胎里</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>枯燥了</rb><rt>kwʽu-sao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>笃实</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>送掉</rb><rt>song-diao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。

### Lm. V.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>相和了</rb><rt>siang-ʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>地位</rb><rt>di-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>患难</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>患难</rb><rt>wæn-næn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih*</rt><rb>习练</rb><rt>jih-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>习练</rb><rt>jih-lin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih*</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>爱惜心</rb><rt>æ-sih-sing</rt><rb>浇</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒力量</rb><rt>m-lih-liang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>*ding-kwe-go*</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>待等</rb><rt>Dæ-teng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>难得</rb><rt>næn-teh</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>恩人</rb><rt>eng-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽO-hwông</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>将来个</rb><rt>*tsiang-læ-go*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>相和了</rb><rt>siang-ʽo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>和拢了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>和拢个</rb><rt>ʽo-long-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>SÔ-YI</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来东了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>犯法个</rb><rt>væn-fah-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>后来</rb><rt>ʽeo-læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>印证</rb><rt>ing-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, dzæ-ü gyi. 有得翻，在于其。（原注）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>赏赐</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>茂盛</rb><rt>meo-zing</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>赏赐</rb><rt>sông-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>*ko-væn*</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>恩赏</rb><rt>eng-sông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>*lih-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>*lih-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>作出来</rb><rt>tsôh-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>多个</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>多起来了</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lm. VI.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>天父个</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>合拢</rb><rt>ʽeh-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>*yiang-shih*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>合拢</rb><rt>*ʽeh-long*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>旧人</rb><rt>gyiu-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>罪恶个</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>脱出了</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng*, tʽeh-cʽih ze sön tsing-dzih. 本文，脱出罪算正直。（原注）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>*üong-yün*</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>*sön*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>肌身个</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>非礼个</rb><rt>fi-li-go</rt><rb>家生</rb><rt>kô-sang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>*pô*</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>家生</rb><rt>kô-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>Ze</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kyüing-kyʽi. 或话，君器。（原注）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>*nu-boh*</rt><rb>归于</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>依顺个</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>*nu-boh*</rt><rb>归于</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>多谢</rb><rt>To-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>道理个</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>规模</rb><rt>kwe-mo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>释放了</rb><rt>sih-fông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>世情</rb><rt>shü-dzing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>秽污</rb><rt>w̆e-u</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非礼个</rb><rt>fi-li-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>惶恐个</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干个</rb><rt>z-ken-go</rt><rb>归根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>归根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Tsiao* *Peng-veng*, *dao-li* *z* *tông* *su-ts*, *i-jing* *dao-li* *cü-kwu* *ziang* *yiang-ko-liao-go* *dong-shü* *tao* *læ* *su-ts* *li-hyiang* *cü-cʽih-go* *ka*. 照本文，道理是当塑子，依顺道理主顾像炀过了个铜水倒来塑子里向铸出个介。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ih-ngæn m-neh kong-yi. 或话，一眼呒得公义。（原注）</sup>

### Lm. VII.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>结拢了个</rb><rt>kyih-long-liao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>男𭑸个</rb><rt>nen-nyüing-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>奸妇</rb><rt>kæn-vu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>奸妇</rb><rt>kæn-vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>*yüong-wô*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肉身里</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-li</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>*fah-cʽih-læ*</rt><rb>罪恶个</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行动</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>归于</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>服个</rb><rt>voh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>脱出了</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>文字个</rb><rt>veng-z-go</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>灵气个</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>*fah-ts*</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *wô*, ah-lah z tʽeh-cʽih lih-fah, feh voh gyi kæ-kwun; ing-we, kông-tao sô kwun-djü ah-lah, z yi-kying si-de. 有得话，阿拉是脱出律法，弗服该管；因为——讲到所管住阿拉——是已经死了。（原注）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>贪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>贪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>趁此机会</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ-kyi-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇条</rb><rt>keh-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>贪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇条</rb><rt>keh-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇条</rb><rt>keh-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>趁此机会</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ-kyi-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇条</rb><rt>keh-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一条</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>*si*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>*lih-fah*</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇条</rb><rt>keh-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>极头</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>刻忖个</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>*læ-tih*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>打算弗出</rb><rt>tang-sön-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>*læ-tih*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>里头个</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>中意</rb><rt>cong-i</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>灵性个</rb><rt>ling-sing-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>争斗</rb><rt>tsang-teo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>感谢</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>*tʽeh-diao*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, iao si go kyi-sing. 或话，要死个肌身。（原注）</sup>

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>*voh*</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。
