### Lm. VIII.

<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>SÔ-YI</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毫无</rb><rt>ʽao-vu</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>呒力量</rb><rt>m-lih-liang</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>*yi-kying*</rt><rb>做过了</rb><rt>*tso-ko-de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>成了</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>有罪个</rb><rt>yiu-ze-go</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>赎罪个</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>*liu-sing*</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>留心</rb><rt>Liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>*voh*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, we-leh ng-lah li-hyiang deng-tih Gyi-zi-go Ling, we s-teh ng-lah iao si go kyi-sing yia hao weh. 或话，为了尔拉里向庉的其自个灵，会使得尔拉要死个肌身也好活。（原注）</sup>

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>KA</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>肌身个</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>引导</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>领子个</rb><rt>ling-ts-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>阿爸</rb><rt>Ah-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>*tso-leh*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接代个</rb><rt>tsih-dæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>接代</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接代个</rb><rt>tsih-dæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>同</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>*tsiang-læ*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>同</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>享</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>今世个</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt><rb>比弗得</rb><rt>pi-feh-teh</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>造出来个</rb><rt>Zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>造出来个</rb><rt>Zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>虚花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>造出来个</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>难头</rb><rt>*næn-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>儿囡个</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>释放个</rb><rt>sih-fông-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>造出来个</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>嗐声叹气</rb><rt>ʽæ-sing-tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>生产个</rb><rt>seng-tsʽæn-go</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>嗐声叹气</rb><rt>ʽæ-sing-tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>领子个</rb><rt>ling-ts-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>取赎个</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>眼见个</rb><rt>ngæn-kyin-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, væn-veh. 或话，万物。（原注）</sup>

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>软弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>求法</rb><rt>gyiu-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>讲弗出个</rb><rt>kông-feh-cʽih-go</rt><rb>叹息</rb><rt>tʽæn-sih</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing-Ling*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>*ts-i*</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>归拢</rb><rt>kwe-long</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>形象</rb><rt>ying-ziang</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>长子</rb><rt>Tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>？<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>状</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> （<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>全日</rb><rt>djün-nyih</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」 ### Lm. IX.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真话</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讲假话</rb><rt>kông-kô-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲族个</rb><rt>tsʽing-dzoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>绝开</rb><rt>djih-kʽæ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>领子个</rb><rt>ling-ts-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>光明个</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>*shih-lih-go*</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐落个</rb><rt>s-lôh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>礼节</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>万物之上</rb><rt>væn-veh-ts-zông</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, be tsiu-mô-go, teng Kyi-toh djih-kʽæ. 或话，被咒骂个，等<u>基督</u>绝开。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, yi-coh. 或话，遗嘱。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算数</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，」 <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>Fi-dæn</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>*na-hwun*</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好歹</rb><rt>hao-tæ</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>做出来</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>拣选个</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算数</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。）<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*CÜ*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>大个</rb><rt>Do-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以扫</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>恶愫个</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>奔竞</rb><rt>peng-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>哀怜心</rb><rt>æ-lin-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>举</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>遍天下</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>扬开去</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>*æ-lin*</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>*ngang*</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>*sing-dziang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>责备</rb><rt>tsah-be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>阻勒住</rb><rt>tsu-leh-djü</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>Ng-noh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>做出个</rb><rt>Tso-cʽih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>Na-nyi</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>烧窑</rb><rt>siao-yiao</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做团</rb><rt>tso-dön</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>*tso-cʽih*</rt><rb>卑贱</rb><rt>pe-zin</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>*kyʽi-ming*</rt></ruby>？<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>*ʽO-hwông*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt><rb>宽恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>配办</rb><rt>pʽe-bæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，——<sup>23, 24</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>富足个</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>*soh-go*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>*ni*</rt></ruby>？

<sup>\[1\] Pæn-poh; *ʽôh* *wô*, ing-cü. 扳驳，或话，应嘴。（原注）</sup>

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>何西</u>个</rb><rt>ʽO-si-go</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>*dzong-zin*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讴勒响</rb><rt>eo-leh-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海里个</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-tong</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>正直无私</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-vu-s</rt><rb>了结</rb><rt>liao-kyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>*Yi-seh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>了结</rb><rt>liao-kyih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上文</rb><rt>zông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>天兵天将个</rb><rt>tʽin-ping-tʽin-tsiang-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>变了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Iao kyüih-tön *Gyi-go* su-moh. 或话，要决断其个数目。（原注）</sup>

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>tse-gyiu</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>tse-gyiu</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>赶着</rb><rt>ken-djôh</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>追求</rb><rt>*tse-gyiu*</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撞着</rb><rt>djông-djôh</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>𢲔跌个</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>𢲔跌个</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>关碍</rb><rt>kwæn-ngæ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>山上</rb><rt>Shing-sæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」 

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tao-me. 或话，倒霉。（原注）</sup>
