### Lm. XVI.

<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓比</rb><rt>Fi-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>坚革哩</rb><rt>Kyin-keh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教会个</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>办事人</rb><rt>bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>托托</rb><rt>tʽôh-tʽôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣徒个</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>*pông-dzu*</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>差一眼</rb><rt>tsʽô-ih-ngæn</rt><rb>候勒</rb><rt>ʽeo-leh</rt><rb>刀口里</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>感谢</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>*mông-mông*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拜尼土</rb><rt>Yi-pæ-nyi-tʽu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚该亚</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>劳心费力</rb><rt>lao-sing-fi-lih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安多尼古</rb><rt>En-to-nyi-kwu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹尼亚</rb><rt>Yiu-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>落监个</rb><rt>lôh-kæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>Mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>暗伯利</rb><rt>En-pah-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>Mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耳巴奴</rb><rt>R-pô-nu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士大古</rb><rt>Z-da-kwu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>致致诚诚</rb><rt>ts-ts-dzing-dzing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>嗅嘴个</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Keh* *liang* *tsih*, keh-kô *liang-go* *z*, *yiu-teh* *wô*, beng-yiu. 箇两节，「箇家」两个字，有得话，「朋友」。（原注）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>学过个</rb><rt>ʽôh-ko-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>害累</rb><rt>ʽæ-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>避开</rb><rt>bi-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇株</rb><rt>Keh-cü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>*voh-z*</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>甜糖蜜渧</rb><rt>din-dông-mih-ti</rt><rb>奉承</rb><rt>vong-dzing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>哄诱</rb><rt>hong-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>依顺个</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>*ming-sing*</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>老实</rb><rt>lao-jih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>和平个</rb><rt>ʽo-bing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>踏糊</rb><rt>dah-wu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nyü-beng. 或话，愚笨。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sông-ʽæ læ ng-lah kyiah-ʽô: *kʽen* Ts. 3. 15. 或话，伤害来尔拉脚下。看 Ts. 3. 15.（原注）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路求</rb><rt>Lu-gyiu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶孙</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>所西巴德</rb><rt>Sô-si-pô-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>代写</rb><rt>dæ-sia</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>德丢</rb><rt>Teh-tiu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦犹</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教会里个</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>县里个</rb><rt>Yün-li-go</rt><rb>库房</rb><rt>kwʽu-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉都</rb><rt>Yi-lah-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>括土</rb><rt>Kwah-tʽu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奥妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永古以来</rb><rt>üong-kwu-yi-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隐藏的</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>表</rb><rt>piao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>万国人</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt></ruby>。）<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>独一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
